a4:T6b0,// Records for things with fixed fields, dicts for things found by name,
// lists for things in order, and functions where each entry has its
// own rule.

type Room {
    teacher: String
    students: [String]
    marks: {String: [Int]}          // each subject's marks, in student order
}

type School {
    name: String
    rooms: {String: Room}           // by class: "3rd", "4th", …
}

fn primary_fee(students: Int) -> Int {
    students * 1200
}

fn average(marks: [Int]) -> Float {
    if marks.is_empty() {
        return 0.0
    }
    Float(marks.sum(m => m)) / Float(marks.len())
}

fn main() {
    const school = School(
        name: "Sunrise School",
        rooms: {
            "3rd": Room(
                teacher: "Mrs. Rao",
                students: ["Asha", "Ravi"],
                marks: {"math": [78, 92], "art": [88, 70]},
            ),
            "4th": Room(teacher: "Mr. Das", students: ["Zoe", "Kabir"], marks: {"math": [65, 81]}),
        },
    )

    // Each class's fee rule: a named function, or a lambda written here.
    const fees: {String: fn(Int) -> Int} = {
        "3rd": primary_fee,
        "4th": n => n * 1500 + 2000,
    }

    print(school.name)
    for (class_name, room) in school.rooms {
        const math = average(room.marks["math"] ?? [])
        print(`{class_name}: {room.teacher}, {room.students.len()} students, math {math}`)
        if let fee = fees[class_name] {
            print(`  fees: {fee(room.students.len())}`)
        }
    }

    // Reach deep: a class, then a subject, then one student's mark.
    if let room = school.rooms["3rd"] {
        const art = room.marks["art"] ?? []
        print(`Ravi's art mark: {art.get(1) ?? 0}`)
    }

109:T898,type Shelf {
    numbers: [Int]
    names: [String]
    tags: Set<String>
    ages: {String: Int}
    recent: Ring<Int, max: 3>
}

fn main() {
    const shelf = Shelf(
        numbers: [5, 2, 8, 2],
        names: ["pen", "ink"],
        tags: Set.of("new", "cheap"),
        ages: {"asha": 31, "ravi": 27},
        recent: Ring<Int, max: 3>([1, 2, 3, 4]),
    )
    const n = shelf.numbers
    print(`len {n.len()}, is_empty {n.is_empty()}, is_full {n.is_full()}`)
    print(`first {n.first() ?? 0}, last {n.last() ?? 0}, get(1) {n.get(1) ?? 0}, n[1] {n[1]}`)
    print(`contains 8: {n.contains(8)}, index_of 2: {n.index_of(2) ?? -1}, count 2s: {n.count(x => x == 2)}`)
    print(`find_index {n.find_index(x => x == 2) ?? -1}, find_last_index {n.find_last_index(x => x == 2) ?? -1}`)
    n[:1].for_each(x => print(`for_each {x}`))
    print(`min {n.min() ?? 0}, max {n.max() ?? 0}, sum {n.sum(x => x)}, find {n.find(x => x > 4) ?? 0}`)
    print(`map {n.map(x => x * 10)}, filter {n.filter(x => x > 2)}, flat_map {n.flat_map(x => [x, x])}`)
    print(`any {n.any(x => x > 7)}, all {n.all(x => x > 0)}, enumerate {n[:2].enumerate()}`)
    print(`sort {n.sort()}, sort_by {n.sort_by(x => -x)}, reversed {n[::-1]}`)
    print(`first two {n[:2]}, after two {n[2:]}, slice {n[1:3]}, + {n + [9]}`)
    print(`join {shelf.names.join(", ")}`)
    print(`still {shelf.numbers}`)
    print(`set: {shelf.tags.contains("new")} {shelf.tags.len()} {shelf.tags.is_empty()} {shelf.tags.is_full()} {shelf.tags.to_list()}`)
    const t = shelf.tags
    print(`set: {t.any(x => x == "new")} {t.all(x => x.len() > 2)} {t.count(x => x != "new")} {t.sum(x => x.len())} {t.find(x => x.len() > 3) ?? "-"}`)
    print(`set: {t.map(x => x.len())} {t.filter(x => x != "new")}`)
    t.for_each(x => print(`set for_each {x}`))
    print(`dict: {shelf.ages["asha"] ?? 0} {shelf.ages.get("ravi") ?? 0} {shelf.ages.len()} {shelf.ages.is_empty()} {shelf.ages.is_full()}`)
    print(`dict: {shelf.ages.contains_key("asha")} {shelf.ages.keys()} {shelf.ages.values()}`)
    const r = shelf.recent
    print(`ring: {r.len()} {r.is_empty()} {r.is_full()} {r.first() ?? 0} {r.last() ?? 0} {r.get(0) ?? 0} {r.contains(4)} {r.to_list()}`)
